~~~text
---
name: verification-before-completion
description: Use when about to claim work is complete, fixed, or passing, before committing or creating PRs - requires running verification commands and confirming output before making any success claims; evidence before assertions always
---

# Verification Before Completion

## Overview

**Core principle:** Evidence before claims, always.

**Violating the letter of this rule is violating the spirit of this rule.**

## The Iron Law

```
NO COMPLETION CLAIMS WITHOUT FRESH VERIFICATION EVIDENCE
```

If you haven't run the verification command in this message, you cannot claim it passes.

## The Gate Function

```
BEFORE claiming any status or expressing satisfaction:

1. IDENTIFY: What command proves this claim?
2. RUN: Execute the FULL command (fresh, complete)
3. READ: Full output, check exit code, count failures
4. VERIFY: Does output confirm the claim?
   - If NO: State actual status with evidence
   - If YES: State claim WITH evidence
5. ONLY THEN: Make the claim

Skip any step = lying, not verifying
```

## Common Failures

| Claim | Requires | Not Sufficient |
|-------|----------|----------------|
| Tests pass | Test command output: 0 failures | Previous run, "should pass" |
| Linter clean | Linter output: 0 errors | Partial check, extrapolation |
| Build succeeds | Build command: exit 0 | Linter passing, logs look good |
| Bug fixed | Test original symptom: passes | Code changed, assumed fixed |
| Regression test works | Red-green cycle verified | Test passes once |
| Agent completed | VCS diff shows changes | Agent reports "success" |
| Requirements met | Line-by-line checklist | Tests passing |

## Red Flags - STOP

- Using "should", "probably", "seems to"
- Expressing satisfaction before verification ("Great!", "Perfect!", "Done!", etc.)
- About to commit/push/PR without verification
- Trusting agent success reports
- Relying on partial verification
- Thinking "just this once"
- Tired and wanting work over
- **ANY wording implying success without having run verification**

## Rationalization Prevention

| Excuse | Reality |
|--------|---------|
| "Should work now" | RUN the verification |
| "I'm confident" | Confidence ≠ evidence |
| "Just this once" | No exceptions |
| "Linter passed" | Linter ≠ compiler |
| "Agent said success" | Verify independently |
| "I'm tired" | Exhaustion ≠ excuse |
| "Partial check is enough" | Partial proves nothing |
| "Different words so rule doesn't apply" | Spirit over letter |

## Key Patterns

**Tests:**
```
✅ [Run test command] [See: 34/34 pass] "All tests pass"
❌ "Should pass now" / "Looks correct"
```

**Regression tests (TDD Red-Green):**
```
✅ Write → Run (pass) → Revert fix → Run (MUST FAIL) → Restore → Run (pass)
❌ "I've written a regression test" (without red-green verification)
```

**Build:**
```
✅ [Run build] [See: exit 0] "Build passes"
❌ "Linter passed" (linter doesn't check compilation)
```

**Requirements:**
```
✅ Re-read plan → Create checklist → Verify each → Report gaps or completion
❌ "Tests pass, phase complete"
```

**Agent delegation:**
```
✅ Agent reports success → Check VCS diff → Verify changes → Report actual state
❌ Trust agent report
```

## When To Apply

**ALWAYS before:**
- ANY variation of success/completion claims
- ANY expression of satisfaction
- ANY positive statement about work state
- Committing, PR creation, task completion
- Moving to next task
- Delegating to agents

**Rule applies to:**
- Exact phrases
- Paraphrases and synonyms
- Implications of success
- ANY communication suggesting completion/correctness

~~~

   191	    def chunk(kind, payload):
   192	        return struct.pack(">I", len(payload)) + kind + payload + struct.pack(">I", zlib.crc32(kind + payload))

   194	    header = struct.pack(">2I5B", 1, 1, 8, 2, 0, 0, 0)

   515	    def kill_isolated_daemon():
   516	        """终止本探针 PID 命名空间内的原生宿主，保留未完成回调边界。"""
   517	        metadata = json.loads((state / "app-server-daemon/daemon.pid").read_text())
   518	        process = metadata["pid"]
   519	        descriptor = os.pidfd_open(process)
   520	        try:
   521	            argv = (Path("/proc") / str(process) / "cmdline").read_bytes().split(b"\0")
   522	            assert b"app-server" in argv and Path(os.fsdecode(argv[0])).name == "codex", argv
   523	            signal.pidfd_send_signal(descriptor, signal.SIGKILL)
   524	            assert select.select([descriptor], [], [], 10)[0], "隔离宿主未退出"

    "text": "{\"i\":3,\"code\":0,\"output\":\"---\\nname: verification-before-completion\\ndescription: Use when about to claim work is complete, fixed, or passing, before committing or creating PRs - requires running verification commands and confirming output before making any success claims; evidence before assertions always\\n---\\n\\n# Verification Before Completion\\n\\n## Overview\\n\\n**Core principle:** Evidence before claims, always.\\n\\n**Violating the letter of this rule is violating the spirit of this rule.**\\n\\n## The Iron Law\\n\\n```\\nNO COMPLETION CLAIMS WITHOUT FRESH VERIFICATION EVIDENCE\\n```\\n\\nIf you haven't run the verification command in this message, you cannot claim it passes.\\n\\n## The Gate Function\\n\\n```\\nBEFORE claiming any status or expressing satisfaction:\\n\\n1. IDENTIFY: What command proves this claim?\\n2. RUN: Execute the FULL command (fresh, complete)\\n3. READ: Full output, check exit code, count failures\\n4. VERIFY: Does output confirm the claim?\\n   - If NO: State actual status with evidence\\n   - If YES: State claim WITH evidence\\n5. ONLY THEN: Make the claim\\n\\nSkip any step = lying, not verifying\\n```\\n\\n## Common Failures\\n\\n| Claim | Requires | Not Sufficient |\\n|-------|----------|----------------|\\n| Tests pass | Test command output: 0 failures | Previous run, \\\"should pass\\\" |\\n| Linter clean | Linter output: 0 errors | Partial check, extrapolation |\\n| Build succeeds | Build command: exit 0 | Linter passing, logs look good |\\n| Bug fixed | Test original symptom: passes | Code changed, assumed fixed |\\n| Regression test works | Red-green cycle verified | Test passes once |\\n| Agent completed | VCS diff shows changes | Agent reports \\\"success\\\" |\\n| Requirements met | Line-by-line checklist | Tests passing |\\n\\n## Red Flags - STOP\\n\\n- Using \\\"should\\\", \\\"probably\\\", \\\"seems to\\\"\\n- Expressing satisfaction before verification (\\\"Great!\\\", \\\"Perfect!\\\", \\\"Done!\\\", etc.)\\n- About to commit/push/PR without verification\\n- Trusting agent success reports\\n- Relying on partial verification\\n- Thinking \\\"just this once\\\"\\n- Tired and wanting work over\\n- **ANY wording implying success without having run verification**\\n\\n## Rationalization Prevention\\n\\n| Excuse | Reality |\\n|--------|---------|\\n| \\\"Should work now\\\" | RUN the verification |\\n| \\\"I'm confident\\\" | Confidence ≠ evidence |\\n| \\\"Just this once\\\" | No exceptions |\\n| \\\"Linter passed\\\" | Linter ≠ compiler |\\n| \\\"Agent said success\\\" | Verify independently |\\n| \\\"I'm tired\\\" | Exhaustion ≠ excuse |\\n| \\\"Partial check is enough\\\" | Partial proves nothing |\\n| \\\"Different words so rule doesn't apply\\\" | Spirit over letter |\\n\\n## Key Patterns\\n\\n**Tests:**\\n```\\n✅ [Run test command] [See: 34/34 pass] \\\"All tests pass\\\"\\n❌ \\\"Should pass now\\\" / \\\"Looks correct\\\"\\n```\\n\\n**Regression tests (TDD Red-Green):**\\n```\\n✅ Write → Run (pass) → Revert fix → Run (MUST FAIL) → Restore → Run (pass)\\n❌ \\\"I've written a regression test\\\" (without red-green verification)\\n```\\n\\n**Build:**\\n```\\n✅ [Run build] [See: exit 0] \\\"Build passes\\\"\\n❌ \\\"Linter passed\\\" (linter doesn't check compilation)\\n```\\n\\n**Requirements:**\\n```\\n✅ Re-read plan → Create checklist → Verify each → Report gaps or completion\\n❌ \\\"Tests pass, phase complete\\\"\\n```\\n\\n**Agent delegation:**\\n```\\n✅ Agent reports success → Check VCS diff → Verify changes → Report actual state\\n❌ Trust agent report\\n```\\n\\n## When To Apply\\n\\n**ALWAYS before:**\\n- ANY variation of success/completion claims\\n- ANY expression of satisfaction\\n- ANY positive statement about work state\\n- Committing, PR creation, task completion\\n- Moving to next task\\n- Delegating to agents\\n\\n**Rule applies to:**\\n- Exact phrases\\n- Paraphrases and synonyms\\n- Implications of success\\n- ANY communication suggesting completion/correctness\\n\"}"

   319	def completed_item(item, location, events, fallback_inputs, during_commit, code_calls, withheld):

    50	class Responses(http.server.BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
    51	    """固定回包端点在请求到达时读回测试收据。"""
    52	
    53	    def log_message(self, *_args):
    54	        pass
    55	
    56	    def do_GET(self):
    57	        payload = b'{"object":"list","data":[]}'
    58	        self.send_response(200)
    59	        self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json")
    60	        self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(payload)))
    61	        self.end_headers()
    62	        self.wfile.write(payload)
    63	
    64	    def do_POST(self):
    65	        body = self.rfile.read(int(self.headers.get("Content-Length", "0")))
    66	        receipt = self.server.records / "first-prompt.txt"
    67	        request = {
    68	            "case": self.server.case,
    69	            "path": self.path,
    70	            "atNs": time.monotonic_ns(),
    71	            "savedPrompt": receipt.read_text() if receipt.exists() else None,
    72	            "hasFirstPrompt": FIRST_PROMPT.encode() in body,
    73	        }
    74	        self.server.requests.append(request)
    75	        response_id = "response-" + uuid.uuid4().hex
    76	        message = {
    77	            "type": "message", "role": "assistant", "id": response_id + "-message",
    78	            "content": [{"type": "output_text", "text": final_reply(request["case"])}],
    79	        }
    80	        first = {"type": "response.created", "response": {"id": response_id}}
    81	        end = [
    82	            {"type": "response.output_item.done", "item": message},
    83	            {"type": "response.completed", "response": {
    84	                "id": response_id,
    85	                "usage": {"input_tokens": 1, "output_tokens": 1, "total_tokens": 2},
    86	            }},
    87	        ]
    88	        self.send_response(200)
    89	        self.send_header("Content-Type", "text/event-stream")
    90	        self.send_header("Connection", "close")
    91	        self.end_headers()
    92	        try:
    93	            self.write_event(first)
    94	            if request["case"] == "interrupt":
    95	                time.sleep(4)
    96	            for data in end:
    97	                self.write_event(data)
    98	        except (BrokenPipeError, ConnectionResetError):
    99	            pass

   101	    def write_event(self, data):
   102	        self.wfile.write(("data: " + json.dumps(data) + "\n\n").encode())
   103	        self.wfile.flush()

   107	    """运行正常的 Codex 终端入口，响应终端能力查询并保留合成屏幕。"""

   178	async def discover_project_items(codex, repo, method, params):
   179	    """从实际宿主取回项目级入口和状态。"""
   180	    process = await asyncio.create_subprocess_exec(
   181	        codex, "app-server", "--listen", "stdio://", cwd=repo,
   182	        stdin=asyncio.subprocess.PIPE, stdout=asyncio.subprocess.PIPE,
   183	        stderr=asyncio.subprocess.DEVNULL,
   184	    )
   185	
   186	    async def send(packet):
   187	        process.stdin.write((json.dumps(packet) + "\n").encode())
   188	        await process.stdin.drain()
   189	
   190	    async def receive(request_id):
   191	        while line := await process.stdout.readline():
   192	            packet = json.loads(line)
   193	            if packet.get("id") == request_id:
   194	                if "error" in packet:
   195	                    raise RuntimeError(packet["error"])
   196	                return packet["result"]
   197	        raise RuntimeError(f"宿主未返回 {method}")
   198	
   199	    try:
   200	        await send({"id": 1, "method": "initialize", "params": {
   201	            "clientInfo": {"name": "foundry-t004", "version": "1"},
   202	        }})
   203	        await asyncio.wait_for(receive(1), 10)
   204	        await send({"method": "initialized"})
   205	        await send({"id": 2, "method": method, "params": params})
   206	        return (await asyncio.wait_for(receive(2), 10))["data"]
   207	    finally:
   208	        process.stdin.close()
   209	        try:
   210	            await asyncio.wait_for(process.wait(), 3)
   211	        except asyncio.TimeoutError:
   212	            process.kill()
   213	            await process.wait()
   214	
   215	
   216	async def list_hooks(codex, repo, scopes):
   217	    """使用与 /hooks 相同的宿主发现接口读取候选和实际信任状态。"""
   218	    return await discover_project_items(codex, repo, "hooks/list", {"cwds": [str(scope) for scope in scopes]})

Usage: codex app-server generate-json-schema [OPTIONS] --out <DIR>

Options:
  -c, --config <key=value>
          Override a configuration value that would otherwise be loaded from `~/.codex/config.toml`.
          Use a dotted path (`foo.bar.baz`) to override nested values. The `value` portion is parsed
          as TOML. If it fails to parse as TOML, the raw string is used as a literal.

server.gates[label].set()\n\n\ndef child_finish(label):\n    row = wait(lambda: rollout_row(\"task_complete\", \"last_agent_message\", CHILD_REPLY[label]),\n               f\"子代理 {label} 实际结束\")\n encoding=\"utf-8\")\n\n    with http.server.ThreadingHTTPServer((\"127.0.0.1\", 0), Endpoint) as server:\n        server.gates = {name: threading.Event() for name in (\"A\", \"B\", false\nrequest_max_retries = 0\nstream_max_retries = 0\n''', encoding=\"utf-8\")\n        try:\n            assert not hooks()\n            start()\n            wait(lambda: \"Trust and continue\" in screen(), \"首次项目目录信任\")\n            time.sleep(0.32)\n            tmux(\"send-keys\", \"-t\", \"timeline\", \"Enter\")\n            wait(lambda: \"Trust all and continue\" in screen(), \"首次 Hook 信任\")\n            candidates = hooks()\n            assert candidates and all(entry[\"trustStatus\"] == \"untrusted\" for entry in candidates)\n            tmux(\"send-keys\", \"-t\", \"timeline\", \"Down\")\n            wait(lambda: \"› 2. Trust all\" in screen(), \"临时 Hook 待确认\")\n            tmux(\"send-keys\", \"-t\", \"timeline\", \"Enter\")\n            wait(lambda: \"gpt-5.4\" in screen(), \"临时项目 Codex 就绪\")\n            trusted = hooks()\n            assert len(candidates) == len(trusted)\n            assert all(entry[\"trustStatus\"] == \"trusted\" and entry[\"enabled\"]\n                       and Path(entry[\"sourcePath\"]) == hooks_path for entry in trusted), trusted\n            append(\"NativeTrust\", hooks=[entry[\"eventName\"] for entry in trusted])\n            end()\n            assert not records_snapshot(repo), \"空白引导会话创建了记录\"\n\n            start()\n            wait(lambda: \"gpt-5.4\" in screen(), \"空白引导后普通启动\")\n            send(PARENT_PROMPT[\"first\"])\n            for label in CHILD_INPUT:\n                wait(lambda: opened_requests(label), f\"子代理 {label} 已收到合成输入\")\n            parent = read_back(\"first\")\n            assert PARENT_REPLY[\"first\"].split(\"\\n\")[-1] in screen()\n            assert rollout_row(\"task_complete\", \"last_agent_message\", PARENT_REPLY[\"first\"], parent.stem)\n            assert all(not server.gates[label].is_set() for label in CHILD_INPUT)\n            assert all(not rollout_row(\"task_complete\", \"last_agent_message\", text)\n                       for text in CHILD_REPLY.values())\n            checkpoint(\"parent-first-done\")\n\n            gate_release(server, \"A\")\n            child_finish(\"A\")\n            time.sleep(1.5)\n            checkpoint(\"child-a-parent-idle\")\n\n            send(PARENT_PROMPT[\"second\"])\n            wait(lambda: opened_requests(\"second\"), \"第二轮已抵达父模型，用户原文已进入宿主\")\n            assert formal_message(\"发起\", PARENT_PROMPT[\"second\"]) in parent.read_text()\n            assert not parent_record(\"second\")\n            assert not rollout_row(\"task_complete\", \"last_agent_message\", CHILD_REPLY[\"B\"])\n            checkpoint(\"parent-second-started\")\n            gate_release(server, \"B\")\n            child_finish(\"B\")\n            time.sleep(0.4)\n            checkpoint(\"child-b-parent-second-active\")\n            gate_release(server, checkpoint(\"parent-second-done\")\n            end()\n\n            start()\n            wait(lambda: \"gpt-5.4\" in screen(), \"普通对照会话就绪\")\n            send(PARENT_PROMPT[\"control\"])\n            control = read_back(\"control\")\n            assert control != parent and control.read_text().count(\"<!-- turn-\") == 1\n            assert not list((control.parent / \"details\" / ## 工具调用 `exec` · `call_d902f99b4d3141be869a777e934298c3`

- 来源定位：字节 1312359

~~~text
工作目录：/home/ena/workspace/ena/agent-foundry/feature/init
const work=load("timeline_work");
const result=await tools.exec_command({cmd:`cat > ${work}/probe.py <<'PY'
${String.raw`"""用隔离 Codex CLI 观测合成子代理事件。"""

import asyncio
import hashlib
import http.server
import json
from pathlib import Path
import runpy
import shlex
import subprocess
import sys
import threading
import time
import uuid

WORK = Path(sys.argv[2])
LOG = WORK / "observations.jsonl"
EVENTS = ("UserPromptSubmit", "PreToolUse", "PostToolUse", "SubagentStart", "SubagentStop", "Stop")
PARENT_PROMPT = {"first": "TIMELINE_PARENT_PROMPT_ONE", "second": "TIMELINE_PARENT_PROMPT_TWO",
                 "control": "TIMELINE_CONTROL_PROMPT"}
PARENT_REPLY = {"first": "TIMELINE_PARENT_FINAL_ONE\n\n主代理独立完成首轮。",
                "second": "TIMELINE_PARENT_FINAL_TWO\n\n主代理独立完成第二轮。",
                "control": "TIMELINE_CONTROL_FINAL\n\n普通对话对照。"}
CHILD_INPUT = {"A": "TIMELINE_INPUT_A\n\n业务子代理 A 的合成输入。",
               "B": "TIMELINE_INPUT_B\n\n业务子代理 B 的合成输入。"}
CHILD_REPLY = {"A": "TIMELINE_REPLY_A\n\n业务子代理 A 的实际合成回复。",
               "B": "TIMELINE_REPLY_B\n\n业务子代理 B 的实际合成回复。"}


def append(kind, **values):
    row = {"kind": kind, "monotonicNs": time.monotonic_ns(), "wallNs": time.time_ns(), **values}
    with LOG.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as stream:
        stream.write(json.dumps(row, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(",", ":")) + "\n")


def json_lines(path):
    if not path.exists():
        return []
    return [json.loads(line) for line in path.read_bytes().splitlines(keepends=True) if line.endswith(b"\n")]


if sys.argv[1] == "--observe-hook":
    append("Hook", configuredEvent=sys.argv[3], payload=json.load(sys.stdin))
    print("{}")
    raise SystemExit


BINARY, REPO_SOURCE, MODE = sys.argv[3:]
SOURCE = Path(REPO_SOURCE)
HELPER = SOURCE / "packages/skills/tests/conversation-workflow/helpers/recording-hook-probe.py"
FIXTURE = runpy.run_path(str(HELPER))
Responses = FIXTURE["HostResponses"]
call_item = FIXTURE["Provider"].call
list_hooks = FIXTURE["list_hooks"]
formal_message = FIXTURE["formal_message"]
records_snapshot = FIXTURE["records_snapshot"]
repo = WORK / "repo"
state = WORK / "state"
config_path = state / "config.toml"


def find_tool(tools, name):
    for tool in tools:
        if tool.get("name") == name:
            return None, tool
        for nested in tool.get("tools", []):
            if nested.get("name") == name:
                return tool["name"], nested
    raise LookupError(name)


class Endpoint(Responses):
    def do_POST(self):
        try:
            self.run_request()
        except Exception as error:
            append("RequestError", error=repr(error))
            raise

    def run_request(self):
        request = json.loads(self.rfile.read(int(self.headers["Content-Length"])))
        history = request["input"]
        tool_outputs = {row["call_id"]: row["output"] for row in history
                        if row["type"] == "function_call_output"}
        user_texts = [part.get("text", "") for row in history if row.get("role") == "user"
                      for part in row.get("content", []) if isinstance(part, dict)]
        user_text = "\n".join(user_texts)
        child = next((label for label, value in CHILD_INPUT.items() if value in user_text), None)
        parent = next((label for label in ("control", "second", "first")
                       if PARENT_PROMPT[label] in user_text), None)
        auxiliary = "text" in request or not request.get("tools")
        correlation = child or parent or "other"
        append("Request", correlation=correlation, auxiliary=auxiliary, childInputExact=(
            CHILD_INPUT[child] in user_texts if child else None), toolOutputs=tool_outputs)
        if child and not auxiliary:
            assert self.server.gates[child].wait(50), f"未按时放行子代理 {child}"
            item = self.final(CHILD_REPLY[child])
        elif parent == "first" and not auxiliary:
            missing = next((label for label in CHILD_INPUT if f"input-{label}" not in tool_outputs), None)
            if missing:
                namespace, tool = find_tool(request["tools"], "spawn_agent")
                params = (tool.get("parameters") or {}).get("properties", {})
                args = {"message": CHILD_INPUT[missing]}
                if "task_name" in params:
                    args["task_name"] = f"timeline_{missing.lower()}"
                if "fork_turns" in params:
                    args["fork_turns"] = "none"
                if "fork_context" in params:
                    args["fork_context"] = False
                item = call_item(f"input-{missing}", "spawn_agent", args, namespace)
                append("Dispatch", child=missing, namespace=namespace, arguments=args)
            else:
                item = self.final(PARENT_REPLY[parent])
        elif parent and not auxiliary:
            if parent == "second":
                assert self.server.gates["parent-second"].wait(30), "未按时放行第二轮合成父回复"
            item = self.final(PARENT_REPLY[parent])
        elif auxiliary:
            item = self.final("TIMELINE_SYNTHETIC_AUXILIARY")
        else:
            append("UnexpectedRequest", userTexts=user_texts)
            item = self.final("TIMELINE_UNEXPECTED_REQUEST")
        self.emit(item)
        append("ReplySent", correlation=correlation, auxiliary=auxiliary, type=item["type"])

    @staticmethod
    def final(text):
        return {"id": "msg-" + uuid.uuid4().hex, "type": "message", "role": "assistant",
                "phase": "final_answer", "content": [{"type": "output_text", "text": text}]}

    def emit(self, item):
        response_id = "resp-" + uuid.uuid4().hex
        self.send_response(200)
        self.send_header("Content-Type", "text/event-stream")
        self.send_header("Connection", "close")
        self.end_headers()
        self.write_event({"type": "response.created", "response": {"id": response_id}})
        if item["type"] == "message":
            self.write_event({"type": "response.output_item.added", "output_index": 0,
                              "item": {**item, "content": []}})
            self.write_event({"type": "response.output_text.delta", "output_index": 0,
                              "item_id": item["id"], "content_index": 0,
                              "delta": item["content"][0]["text"]})
        self.write_event({"type": "response.output_item.done", "output_index": 0, "item": item})
        self.write_event({"type": "response.completed", "response": {"id": response_id, "output": [item],
                         "usage": {"input_tokens": 1, "output_tokens": 1, "total_tokens": 2}}})


def git(*args):
    subprocess.run(["git", *args], cwd=repo, check=True, capture_output=True)


def tmux(*args):
    return subprocess.check_output(["tmux", "-f", "/dev/null", "-S", str(WORK / "terminal.sock"), *args],
                                   text=True, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)


def screen():
    try:
        return tmux("capture-pane", "-p", "-S", "-150", "-t", "timeline")
    except subprocess.CalledProcessError as error:
        return error.output


def wait(predicate, description, timeout=15):
    limit = time.monotonic() + timeout
    while time.monotonic() < limit:
        result = predicate()
        if result:
            return result
        time.sleep(0.05)
    raise RuntimeError(f"{description}\n{screen()[-3500:]}\n{json_lines(LOG)[-5:]}")


def start():
    tmux("new-session", "-d", "-s", "timeline", "-x", "125", "-y", "40", "-c", str(repo), BINARY)


def send(text):
    tmux("send-keys", "-t", "timeline", "-l", text)
    wait(lambda: text in screen(), f"终端回显 {text}")
    tmux("send-keys", "-t", "timeline", "Enter")


def end():
    send("/exit")
    wait(lambda: "can't find" in screen() or "no server running" in screen(),
         "终端正常退出")


def hooks():
    return asyncio.run(list_hooks(BINARY, repo, (repo,)))[0]["hooks"]


def opened_requests(correlation):
    return [item for item in json_lines(LOG) if item["kind"] == "Request"
            and item["correlation"] == correlation and not item["auxiliary"]]


def rollout_row(kind, field, value, thread_id=None):
    for path in (state / "sessions").rglob("rollout-*.jsonl"):
        if thread_id and not path.stem.endswith(thread_id):
            continue
        for number, row in enumerate(json_lines(path), start=1):
            if row.get("payload", {}).get("type") == kind and row["payload"].get(field) == value:
                return {"file": path.name, "line": number, **row}
    return None


def parent_record(turn):
    expected = formal_message("回复", PARENT_REPLY[turn])
    roots = (repo / "records", repo / "conversation-recording/records")
    return next((path for root in roots for path in root.glob("*/*.md")
                 if expected in path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")), None)


def read_back(turn):
    path = wait(lambda: parent_record(turn), f"主记录已保存 {turn}")
    contents = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert contents.count(formal_message("回复", PARENT_REPLY[turn])) == 1
    assert contents.count(formal_message("发起", PARENT_PROMPT[turn])) == 1
    append("ParentReadBack", turn=turn, sessionId=path.stem, record=path.relative_to(repo).as_posix(),
           sha256=hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest(), mtimeNs=path.stat().st_mtime_ns,
           snapshot=records_snapshot(repo))
    return path


def gate_release(server, label):
    append("GateRelease", label=label)
    server.gates[label].set()


def child_finish(label):
    row = wait(lambda: rollout_row("task_complete", "last_agent_message", CHILD_REPLY[label]),
               f"子代理 {label} 实际结束")
    append("ChildCompleteReadBack", label=label, **row)
    return row


def checkpoint(label):
    append("Checkpoint", label=label, snapshot=records_snapshot(repo))
    (WORK / f"{label}.screen.txt").write_text(screen(), encoding="utf-8")


def run():
    repo.mkdir()
    git("init", "-q")
    (repo / "README.md").write_text("隔离合成仓库\n", encoding="utf-8")
    git("add", "README.md")
    git("-c", "user.name=Timeline Fixture", "-c", "user.email=fixture@example.invalid",
        "commit", "-qm", "synthetic fixture")
    maintained = SOURCE / "packages/skills/src/profile/conversation-recording"
    subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-B", str(maintained / "install.py"), "--project", str(repo)],
                   cwd=repo, check=True, capture_output=True)
    installed = repo / ".agents/skills/conversation-recording"
    paths = {path.relative_to(maintained).as_posix(): hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
             for path in maintained.rglob("*") if path.is_file()}
    assert paths and all(hashlib.sha256((installed / name).read_bytes()).hexdigest() == digest
                         for name, digest in paths.items())
    append("Install", version=subprocess.check_output([BINARY, "--version"], text=True).strip(),
           mode=MODE, hashes=paths)
    hooks_path = repo / ".codex/hooks.json"
    config = json.loads(hooks_path.read_text())
    installed_events = set(config["hooks"])
    assert installed_events == {"UserPromptSubmit", "PostToolUse", "Stop"}, installed_events
    for event in EVENTS:
        command = shlex.join([sys.executable, "-B", __file__, "--observe-hook", str(WORK), event])
        config["hooks"].setdefault(event, []).append({"hooks": [{"type": "command", "command": command}]})
    hooks_path.write_text(json.dumps(config, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")

    with http.server.ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", 0), Endpoint) as server:
        server.gates = {name: threading.Event() for name in ("A", "B", "parent-second")}
        threading.Thread(target=server.serve_forever, daemon=True).start()
        config_path.write_text(f'''model = "gpt-5.4"
model_provider = "fixture"
check_for_update_on_startup = false
approval_policy = "never"
sandbox_mode = "danger-full-access"
[features]
multi_agent_v2 = {"true" if MODE == "v2" else "false"}
[model_providers.fixture]
name = "Timeline isolated"
base_url = "http://127.0.0.1:{server.server_port}/v1"
wire_api = "responses"
requires_openai_auth = false
request_max_retries = 0
stream_max_retries = 0
''', encoding="utf-8")
        try:
            assert not hooks()
            start()
            wait(lambda: "Trust and continue" in screen(), "首次项目目录信任")
            time.sleep(0.32)
            tmux("send-keys", "-t", "timeline", "Enter")
            wait(lambda: "Trust all and continue" in screen(), "首次 Hook 信任")
            candidates = hooks()
            assert candidates and all(entry["trustStatus"] == "untrusted" for entry in candidates)
            tmux("send-keys", "-t", "timeline", "Down")
            wait(lambda: "› 2. Trust all" in screen(), "临时 Hook 待确认")
            tmux("send-keys", "-t", "timeline", "Enter")
            wait(lambda: "gpt-5.4" in screen(), "临时项目 Codex 就绪")
            trusted = hooks()
            assert len(candidates) == len(trusted)
            assert all(entry["trustStatus"] == "trusted" and entry["enabled"]
                       and Path(entry["sourcePath"]) == hooks_path for entry in trusted), trusted
            append("NativeTrust", hooks=[entry["eventName"] for entry in trusted])
            end()
            assert not records_snapshot(repo), "空白引导会话创建了记录"

            start()
            wait(lambda: "gpt-5.4" in screen(), "空白引导后普通启动")
            send(PARENT_PROMPT["first"])
            for label in CHILD_INPUT:
                wait(lambda: opened_requests(label), f"子代理 {label} 已收到合成输入")
            parent = read_back("first")
            assert PARENT_REPLY["first"].split("\n")[-1] in screen()
            assert rollout_row("task_complete", "last_agent_message", PARENT_REPLY["first"], parent.stem)
            assert all(not server.gates[label].is_set() for label in CHILD_INPUT)
            assert all(not rollout_row("task_complete", "last_agent_message", text)
                       for text in CHILD_REPLY.values())
            checkpoint("parent-first-done")

            gate_release(server, "A")
            child_finish("A")
            time.sleep(1.5)
            checkpoint("child-a-parent-idle")

            send(PARENT_PROMPT["second"])
            wait(lambda: opened_requests("second"), "第二轮已抵达父模型，用户原文已进入宿主")
            assert formal_message("发起", PARENT_PROMPT["second"]) in parent.read_text()
            assert not parent_record("second")
            assert not rollout_row("task_complete", "last_agent_message", CHILD_REPLY["B"])
            checkpoint("parent-second-started")
            gate_release(server, "B")
            child_finish("B")
            time.sleep(0.4)
            checkpoint("child-b-parent-second-active")
            gate_release(server, "parent-second")
            assert read_back("second") == parent
            assert rollout_row("task_complete", "last_agent_message", PARENT_REPLY["second"], parent.stem)
            checkpoint("parent-second-done")
            end()

            start()
            wait(lambda: "gpt-5.4" in screen(), "普通对照会话就绪")
            send(PARENT_PROMPT["control"])
            control = read_back("control")
            assert control != parent and control.read_text().count("<!-- turn-") == 1
            assert not list((control.parent / "details" / control.stem).glob("*.md"))
            assert len(opened_requests("control")) == 1 and not opened_requests("control")[0]["toolOutputs"]
            checkpoint("ordinary-control-done")
            end()
            entries = json_lines(LOG)
            assert [(item["child"], item["arguments"]["message"]) for item in entries
                    if item["kind"] == "Dispatch"] == list(CHILD_INPUT.items())
            assert not any(item["kind"] in ("RequestError", "UnexpectedRequest") for item in entries)
            append("RunCompleted", parentId=parent.stem, controlId=control.stem)
            print(json.dumps({"mode": MODE, "parent": parent.stem, "control": control.stem,
                              "mainRecord": parent.relative_to(repo).as_posix()}, ensure_ascii=False))
        finally:
            (WORK / "last-screen.txt").write_text(screen(), encoding="utf-8")
            for event in server.gates.values():
                event.set()
            try:
                tmux("kill-server")
            except subprocess.CalledProcessError:
                pass
            server.shutdown()


run()
`}

~~~text
---
name: systematic-debugging
description: Use when encountering any bug, test failure, or unexpected behavior, before proposing fixes
---

# Systematic Debugging

## Overview

**Core principle:** ALWAYS find root cause before attempting fixes. Symptom fixes are failure.

**Violating the letter of this process is violating the spirit of debugging.**

## The Iron Law

```
NO FIXES WITHOUT ROOT CAUSE INVESTIGATION FIRST
```

If you haven't completed Phase 1, you cannot propose fixes.

## When to Use

Use for ANY technical issue:
- Test failures
- Bugs in production
- Unexpected behavior
- Performance problems
- Build failures
- Integration issues

**Use this ESPECIALLY when:**
- Under time pressure (emergencies make guessing tempting)
- "Just one quick fix" seems obvious
- You've already tried multiple fixes
- Previous fix didn't work
- You don't fully understand the issue

**Don't skip when:**
- Issue seems simple (simple bugs have root causes too)
- You're in a hurry (rushing guarantees rework)
- Manager wants it fixed NOW (systematic is faster than thrashing)

## The Four Phases

You MUST complete each phase before proceeding to the next.

### Phase 1: Root Cause Investigation

**BEFORE attempting ANY fix:**

1. **Read Error Messages Carefully**
   - Don't skip past errors or warnings
   - They often contain the exact solution
   - Read stack traces completely
   - Note line numbers, file paths, error codes

2. **Reproduce Consistently**
   - Can you trigger it reliably?
   - What are the exact steps?
   - Does it happen every time?
   - If not reproducible → gather more data, don't guess

3. **Check Recent Changes**
   - What changed that could cause this?
   - Git diff, recent commits
   - New dependencies, config changes
   - Environmental differences

4. **Gather Evidence in Multi-Component Systems**

   **WHEN system has multiple components (CI → build → signing, API → service → database):**

   **BEFORE proposing fixes, add diagnostic instrumentation:**
   ```
   For EACH component boundary:
     - Log what data enters component
     - Log what data exits component
     - Verify environment/config propagation
     - Check state at each layer

   Run once to gather evidence showing WHERE it breaks
   THEN analyze evidence to identify failing component
   THEN investigate that specific component
   ```

   **Example (multi-layer system):**
   ```bash
   # Layer 1: Workflow
   echo "=== Secrets available in workflow: ==="
   echo "IDENTITY: $([ -n "${IDENTITY:-}" ] && echo SET || echo UNSET)"

   # Layer 2: Build script
   echo "=== Env vars in build script: ==="
   printenv IDENTITY >/dev/null 2>&1 && echo "IDENTITY in environment" || echo "IDENTITY not in environment"

   # Layer 3: Signing script
   echo "=== Keychain state: ==="
   security list-keychains
   security find-identity -v

   # Layer 4: Actual signing
   codesign --sign "$IDENTITY" --verbose=4 "$APP"
   ```

   **This reveals:** Which layer fails (secrets → workflow ✓, workflow → build ✗)

5. **Trace Data Flow**

   **WHEN error is deep in call stack:**

   See `root-cause-tracing.md` in this directory for the complete backward tracing technique.

   **Quick version:**
   - Where does bad value originate?
   - What called this with bad value?
   - Keep tracing up until you find the source
   - Fix at source, not at symptom

### Phase 2: Pattern Analysis

**Find the pattern before fixing:**

1. **Find Working Examples**
   - Locate similar working code in same codebase
   - What works that's similar to what's broken?

2. **Compare Against References**
   - If implementing pattern, read reference implementation COMPLETELY
   - Don't skim - read every line
   - Understand the pattern fully before applying

3. **Identify Differences**
   - What's different between working and broken?
   - List every difference, however small
   - Don't assume "that can't matter"

4. **Understand Dependencies**
   - What other components does this need?
   - What settings, config, environment?
   - What assumptions does it make?

### Phase 3: Hypothesis and Testing

**Scientific method:**

1. **Form Single Hypothesis**
   - State clearly: "I think X is the root cause because Y"
   - Write it down
   - Be specific, not vague

2. **Test Minimally**
   - Make the SMALLEST possible change to test hypothesis
   - One variable at a time
   - Don't fix multiple things at once

3. **Verify Before Continuing**
   - Did it work? Yes → Phase 4
   - Didn't work? Form NEW hypothesis
   - DON'T add more fixes on top

4. **When You Don't Know**
   - Say "I don't understand X"
   - Don't pretend to know
   - Ask for help
   - Research more

### Phase 4: Implementation

**Fix the root cause, not the symptom:**

1. **Create Failing Test Case**
   - Simplest possible reproduction
   - Automated test if possible
   - One-off test script if no framework
   - MUST have before fixing
   - Use the `superpowers:test-driven-development` skill for writing proper failing tests

2. **Implement Single Fix**
   - Address the root cause identified
   - ONE change at a time
   - No "while I'm here" improvements
   - No bundled refactoring

3. **Verify Fix**
   - Test passes now?
   - No other tests broken?
   - Issue actually resolved?
   - Use the `superpowers:verification-before-completion` skill before claiming success

4. **If Fix Doesn't Work**
   - STOP
   - Count: How many fixes have you tried?
   - If < 3: Return to Phase 1, re-analyze with new information
   - **If ≥ 3: STOP and question the architecture (step 5 below)**
   - DON'T attempt Fix #4 without architectural discussion

5. **If 3+ Fixes Failed: Question Architecture**

   **Pattern indicating architectural problem:**
   - Each fix reveals new shared state/coupling/problem in different place
   - Fixes require "massive refactoring" to implement
   - Each fix creates new symptoms elsewhere

   **STOP and question fundamentals:**
   - Is this pattern fundamentally sound?
   - Are we "sticking with it through sheer inertia"?
   - Should we refactor architecture vs. continue fixing symptoms?

   **Discuss with your human partner before attempting more fixes**

   This is NOT a failed hypothesis - this is a wrong architecture.

## Red Flags - STOP and Follow Process

If you catch yourself thinking:
- "Quick fix for now, investigate later"
- "Just try changing X and see if it works"
- "Add multiple changes, run tests"
- "Skip the test, I'll manually verify"
- "It's probably X, let me fix that"
- "I don't fully understand but this might work"
- "Pattern says X but I'll adapt it differently"
- "Here are the main problems: [lists fixes without investigation]"
- Proposing solutions before tracing data flow
- **"One more fix attempt" (when already tried 2+)**
- **Each fix reveals new problem in different place**

**ALL of these mean: STOP. Return to Phase 1.**

**If 3+ fixes failed:** Question the architecture (see Phase 4.5)

## your human partner's Signals You're Doing It Wrong

**Watch for these redirections:**
- "Is that not happening?" - You assumed without verifying
- "Will it show us...?" - You should have added evidence gathering
- "Stop guessing" - You're proposing fixes without understanding
- "Ultra-think this" - Question fundamentals, not just symptoms
- "We're stuck?" (frustrated) - Your approach isn't working

**When you see these:** STOP. Return to Phase 1.

## Common Rationalizations

| Excuse | Reality |
|--------|---------|
| "Issue is simple, don't need process" | Simple issues have root causes too. Process is fast for simple bugs. |
| "Emergency, no time for process" | Systematic debugging is FASTER than guess-and-check thrashing. |
| "Just try this first, then investigate" | First fix sets the pattern. Do it right from the start. |
| "I'll write test after confirming fix works" | Untested fixes don't stick. Test first proves it. |
| "Multiple fixes at once saves time" | Can't isolate what worked. Causes new bugs. |
| "Reference too long, I'll adapt the pattern" | Partial understanding guarantees bugs. Read it completely. |
| "I see the problem, let me fix it" | Seeing symptoms ≠ understanding root cause. |
| "One more fix attempt" (after 2+ failures) | 3+ failures = architectural problem. Question pattern, don't fix again. |

## Quick Reference

| Phase | Key Activities | Success Criteria |
|-------|---------------|------------------|
| **1. Root Cause** | Read errors, reproduce, check changes, gather evidence | Understand WHAT and WHY |
| **2. Pattern** | Find working examples, compare | Identify differences |
| **3. Hypothesis** | Form theory, test minimally | Confirmed or new hypothesis |
| **4. Implementation** | Create test, fix, verify | Bug resolved, tests pass |

## When Process Reveals "No Root Cause"

If systematic investigation reveals issue is truly environmental, timing-dependent, or external:

1. You've completed the process
2. Document what you investigated
3. Implement appropriate handling (retry, timeout, error message)
4. Add monitoring/logging for future investigation

**But:** 95% of "no root cause" cases are incomplete investigation.

## Supporting Techniques

These techniques are part of systematic debugging and available in this directory:

- **`root-cause-tracing.md`** - Trace bugs backward through call stack to find original trigger
- **`defense-in-depth.md`** - Add validation at multiple layers after finding root cause
- **`condition-based-waiting.md`** - Replace arbitrary timeouts with condition polling

~~~

Issue actually resolved?\\n   - Use the `superpowers:verification-before-completion` skill before claiming success\\n\\n4. **If Fix Doesn't Work**\\n   - STOP\\n   - Count: How many fixes have you tried?\\n   - If < 3: Return to Phase 1, re-analyze with new information\\n   - **If ≥ 3: STOP and question the architecture (step 5 below)**\\n   - DON'T attempt Fix #4 without architectural discussion\\n\\n5. **If 3+ Fixes Failed: Question Architecture**\\n\\n   **Pattern indicating architectural problem:**\\n   - Each fix reveals new shared state/coupling/problem in different place\\n   - Fixes require \\\"massive refactoring\\\" to implement\\n   - Each fix creates new symptoms elsewhere\\n\\n   **STOP and question fundamentals:**\\n   - Is this pattern fundamentally sound?\\n   - Are we \\\"sticking with it through sheer inertia\\\"?\\n   - Should we refactor architecture vs. continue fixing symptoms?\\n\\n   **Discuss with your human partner before attempting more fixes**\\n\\n   This is NOT a failed hypothesis - this is a wrong architecture.\\n\\n## Red Flags - STOP and Follow Process\\n\\nIf you catch yourself thinking:\\n- \\\"Quick fix for now, investigate later\\\"\\n- \\\"Just try changing X and see if it works\\\"\\n- \\\"Add multiple changes, run tests\\\"\\n- \\\"Skip the test, I'll manually verify\\\"\\n- \\\"It's probably X, let me fix that\\\"\\n- \\\"I don't fully understand but this might work\\\"\\n- \\\"Pattern says X but I'll adapt it differently\\\"\\n- \\\"Here are the main problems: [lists fixes without investigation]\\\"\\n- Proposing solutions before tracing data flow\\n- **\\\"One more fix attempt\\\" (when already tried 2+)**\\n- **Each fix reveals new problem in different place**\\n\\n**ALL of these mean: STOP. Return to Phase 1.**\\n\\n**If 3+ fixes failed:** Question the architecture (see Phase 4.5)\\n\\n## your human partner's Signals You're Doing It Wrong\\n\\n**Watch for these redirections:**\\n- \\\"Is that not happening?\\\" - You assumed without verifying\\n- \\\"Will it show us...?\\\" - You should have added evidence gathering\\n- \\\"Stop guessing\\\" - You're proposing fixes without understanding\\n- \\\"Ultra-think this\\\" - Question fundamentals, not just symptoms\\n- \\\"We're stuck?\\\" (frustrated) - Your approach isn't working\\n\\n**When you see these:** STOP. Return to Phase 1.\\n\\n## Common Rationalizations\\n\\n| Excuse | Reality |\\n|--------|---------|\\n| \\\"Issue is simple, don't need process\\\" | Simple issues have root causes too. Process is fast for simple bugs. |\\n| \\\"Emergency, no time for process\\\" | Systematic debugging is FASTER than guess-and-check thrashing. |\\n| \\\"Just try this first, then investigate\\\" | First fix sets the pattern. Do it right from the start. |\\n| \\\"I'll write test after confirming fix works\\\" | Untested fixes don't stick. Test first proves it. |\\n| \\\"Multiple fixes at once saves time\\\" | Can't isolate what worked. Causes new bugs. |\\n| \\\"Reference too long, I'll adapt the pattern\\\" | Partial understanding guarantees bugs. Read it completely. |\\n| \\\"I see the problem, let me fix it\\\" | Seeing symptoms ≠ understanding root cause. |\\n| \\\"One more fix attempt\\\" (after 2+ failures) | 3+ failures = architectural problem. Question pattern, don't fix again. |\\n\\n## Quick Reference\\n\\n| Phase | Key Activities | Success Criteria |\\n|-------|---------------|------------------|\\n| **1. Root Cause** | Read errors, reproduce, check changes, gather evidence | Understand WHAT and WHY |\\n| **2. Pattern** | Find working examples, compare | Identify differences |\\n| **3. Hypothesis** | Form theory, test minimally | Confirmed or new hypothesis |\\n| **4. Implementation** | Create test, fix, verify | Bug resolved, tests pass |\\n\\n## When Process Reveals \\\"No Root Cause\\\"\\n\\nIf systematic investigation reveals issue is truly environmental, timing-dependent, or external:\\n\\n1. You've completed the process\\n2. Document what you investigated\\n3. Implement appropriate handling (retry, timeout, error message)\\n4. Add monitoring/logging for future investigation\\n\\n**But:** 95% of \\\"no root cause\\\" cases are incomplete investigation.\\n\\n## Supporting Techniques\\n\\nThese techniques are part of systematic debugging and available in this directory:\\n\\n- **`root-cause-tracing.md`** - Trace bugs backward through call stack to find original trigger\\n- **`defense-in-depth.md`** - Add validation at multiple layers after finding root cause\\n- **`condition-based-waiting.md`** - Replace arbitrary timeouts with condition polling\\n\"}"
  },

## 工具调用 `exec` · `call_8e286fff4903468c9e2ebec3728c776f`

- 来源定位：字节 1649681

~~~text
工作目录：/home/ena/workspace/ena/agent-foundry/feature/init
const work=load("timeline_work");
const r=await tools.exec_command({cmd:`/usr/bin/python3 -B - ${work} <<'PY'
import json, pathlib, sys
root = pathlib.Path(sys.argv[1])
for mode in ("v2","v1"):
    work=root/f"run-{mode}"
    print(mode)
    for line in (work/"observations.jsonl").read_text().splitlines():
        r=json.loads(line)
        if r["kind"] in ("UnexpectedRequest", "Dispatch"):
            print({k:v for k,v in r.items() if k not in ("monotonicNs","wallNs")})
        if r["kind"]=="Hook":
            p=r["payload"]
            keys=("hook_event_name","session_id","turn_id","tool_name","tool_input","tool_response","agent_id","agent_type","last_assistant_message","prompt")
            print({k:p[k] for k in keys if k in p})
    for path in (work/"state/sessions").rglob("rollout-*.jsonl"):
        print("rollout",path.stem[-36:])
        for n,line in enumerate(path.read_text().splitlines(),1):
            row=json.loads(line)
            p=row["payload"]
            t=p.get("type","")
            if t in ("task_complete","agent_message","user_message","task_started","function_call") or (t=="message" and p.get("role") in ("developer","user") and "TIMELINE_INPUT" in line):
                selection={k:p[k] for k in ("type","role","content","message","last_agent_message","turn_id","name","arguments") if k in p}
                print(n,row["timestamp"],json.dumps(selection,ensure_ascii=False)[:550])
PY`,max_output_tokens:5200});text(r.output);

~~~

const work=load("timeline_work");
const r=await tools.exec_command({cmd:`/usr/bin/python3 -B - ${work} <<'PY'
import json,pathlib,sys
root=pathlib.Path(sys.argv[1])
for mode in ("v2","v1"):
    d=root/f"run-{mode}"
    print(mode)
    rows=list(map(json.loads,(d/"observations.jsonl").read_text().splitlines()))
    for event in ("PreToolUse","PostToolUse","SubagentStart","SubagentStop","UserPromptSubmit"):
        p=next(row["payload"] for row in rows if row.get("configuredEvent")==event)
        print(event,sorted(p),p.get("tool_use_id"),p.get("agent_transcript_path", "").split("/")[-1])
    session=next(r["payload"]["session_id"] for r in rows if r.get("configuredEvent")=="UserPromptSubmit")
    parent=next((d/"state/sessions").rglob(f"*{session}.jsonl"))
    for n,line in enumerate(parent.read_text().splitlines(),1):
        row=json.loads(line)
        p=row["payload"]
        if p.get("type") in ("collab_agent_spawn_begin","collab_agent_spawn_end") or (p.get("item",{}).get("type") in ("SubAgentActivity","CollabAgentToolCall")):
            print(n,row["timestamp"],json.dumps(p,ensure_ascii=False)[:1100])
PY`,max_output_tokens:3400});text(r.output);

runs.forEach((r,i)=>{const mode=["v2","v1"][i];if(r.status==="fulfilled")text({mode,...r.value});else text({mode,error:String(r.reason)});});

const work=load("timeline_work");
const r=await tools.exec_command({cmd:`/usr/bin/python3 -B - ${work} <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime, UTC
import json, sys
root = Path(sys.argv[1])
for mode in ("v2","v1"):
    w=root/f"timeline-{mode}"
    rows=[json.loads(line) for line in (w/"observations.jsonl").read_text().splitlines()]
    details=next(r for r in rows if r["kind"]=="RunCompleted")
    p=details["parentId"]
    print("\\n",mode,p)
    for r in sorted(rows,key=lambda x:x["wallNs"]):
        ts=datetime.fromtimestamp(r["wallNs"]/1e9,UTC).strftime("%H:%M:%S.%f")[:-3]
        kind=r["kind"];a=r.get("payload",{})
        if kind in ("GateRelease","ParentReadBack","ChildCompleteReadBack","Checkpoint"):
            print(ts,kind,r.get("label") or r.get("turn"),r.get("timestamp",""),r.get("sha256","")[:10])
        if kind=="Hook":
            text=a.get("prompt") or a.get("last_assistant_message") or (a.get("tool_input") or {}).get("message","")
            print(ts,r["configuredEvent"],a.get("tool_use_id",""),a.get("turn_id","")[-8:],a.get("agent_id","")[-8:],text.splitlines()[:1])
    path=next((w/"state/sessions").rglob(f"*{p}.jsonl"))
    for n,line in enumerate(path.read_text().splitlines(),1):
        row=json.loads(line);a=row["payload"];item=a.get("item",{}); k=a.get("type")
        if k in ("task_started","task_complete") or item.get("type") in ("SubAgentActivity","CollabAgentToolCall") or (("TIMELINE_REPLY_" in line or "<subagent_notification" in line) and "response_item" in line):
            print(n,row["timestamp"],k,a.get("turn_id","")[-8:],item.get("type",""),item.get("kind",""),item.get("id",""),line[line.find("TIMELINE_REPLY_"):line.find("TIMELINE_REPLY_")+23] if "TIMELINE_REPLY_" in line else "")
    primary=next((w/"repo/records").glob(f"*/{p}.md"))
    print("MAIN",[(word,primary.read_text().count(word)) for word in ("<!-- turn-","TIMELINE_INPUT_A","TIMELINE_INPUT_B","TIMELINE_REPLY_A","TIMELINE_REPLY_B")])
    for screen in ("child-a-parent-idle","child-b-parent-second-active"):
        lines=[l.strip() for l in (w/f"{screen}.screen.txt").read_text().splitlines()]
        print(screen,[l for l in lines if "Completed" in l or "TIMELINE_REPLY_" in l or "finished" in l])
PY`,max_output_tokens:5900});text(r.output);

## 工具调用 `exec` · `call_c603d486e5f249cbaacd4bf09b07de99`

- 来源定位：字节 1881628

~~~text
工作目录：/home/ena/workspace/ena/agent-foundry/feature/init
const work=load("timeline_work");
const r=await tools.exec_command({cmd:`cat > ${work}/extract.py <<'PY'
${String.raw`"""从两次合成隔离运行中提取可复核的事件与快照。"""

from datetime import UTC, datetime
from hashlib import sha256
import json
from pathlib import Path
import sys

WORK = Path(sys.argv[1])


def read_jsonl(path):
    return list(map(json.loads, path.read_text().splitlines()))


def one(values, description):
    matches = list(values)
    assert len(matches) == 1, (description, matches)
    return matches[0]


def as_utc(nanos):
    return datetime.fromtimestamp(nanos / 1e9, UTC).isoformat(timespec="milliseconds").replace("+00:00", "Z")


def references(rows, relative_path, match):
    line, row = one(((index, row) for index, row in enumerate(rows, 1) if match(row)), relative_path)
    return {"file": relative_path, "line": line, "timestamp": row["timestamp"], "payload": row["payload"]}


def main(mode):
    directory = WORK / f"timeline-{mode}"
    observations = read_jsonl(directory / "observations.jsonl")
    finish = one((row for row in observations if row["kind"] == "RunCompleted"), "completed")
    install = one((row for row in observations if row["kind"] == "Install"), "installed")
    trust = one((row for row in observations if row["kind"] == "NativeTrust"), "trusted")
    parent_id = finish["parentId"]
    control_id = finish["controlId"]
    hook_rows = [row for row in observations if row["kind"] == "Hook"]
    parent_file = one((directory / "state/sessions").rglob(f"*{parent_id}.jsonl"), "parent rollout")
    parent_rows = read_jsonl(parent_file)
    parent_path = parent_file.relative_to(directory / "state").as_posix()
    checkpoints = {row["label"]: row for row in observations if row["kind"] == "Checkpoint"}
    reads = {row["turn"]: row for row in observations if row["kind"] == "ParentReadBack"}
    assert reads["first"]["sessionId"] == reads["second"]["sessionId"] == parent_id
    assert reads["control"]["sessionId"] == control_id
    record = directory / "repo" / reads["first"]["record"]
    control = directory / "repo" / reads["control"]["record"]
    assert sha256(record.read_bytes()).hexdigest() == reads["second"]["sha256"]
    assert sha256(control.read_bytes()).hexdigest() == reads["control"]["sha256"]
    assert record.read_text().count("<!-- turn-") == 2
    assert control.read_text().count("<!-- turn-") == 1
    assert control.read_text().count("### 用户 · 发起") == 1 and control.read_text().count("### 主代理 · 回复") == 1
    assert not list((control.parent / "details" / control_id).glob("*.md"))
    assert (checkpoints["parent-first-done"]["snapshot"] == checkpoints["child-a-parent-idle"]["snapshot"])
    assert (checkpoints["parent-second-started"]["snapshot"] == checkpoints["child-b-parent-second-active"]["snapshot"])

    prompt_hooks = [row for row in hook_rows if row["configuredEvent"] == "UserPromptSubmit"
                    and row["payload"]["session_id"] == parent_id
                    and not row["payload"].get("agent_id")]
    assert len(prompt_hooks) == 2, prompt_hooks
    turn_ids = [row["payload"]["turn_id"] for row in prompt_hooks]
    parent_turns = []
    for index, label in enumerate(("first", "second")):
        turn = turn_ids[index]
        started = references(parent_rows, parent_path, lambda row:
                             row["payload"].get("type") == "task_started" and row["payload"].get("turn_id") == turn)
        stopped = one((row for row in hook_rows if row["configuredEvent"] == "Stop"
                       and row["payload"]["turn_id"] == turn), f"parent Stop {label}")
        assert stopped["wallNs"] < reads[label]["wallNs"]
        parent_turns.append({"turnId": turn, "started": {"file": parent_path, "line": started["line"],
                             "time": started["timestamp"]},
                             "promptHookAt": as_utc(prompt_hooks[index]["wallNs"]),
                             "stopHookAt": as_utc(stopped["wallNs"]),
                             "recordReadBackAt": as_utc(reads[label]["wallNs"]),
                             "recordSha256": reads[label]["sha256"]})

    parent_dispatches = [row for row in parent_rows if row["payload"].get("type") in
                         ("function_call", "custom_tool_call", "mcp_tool_call")]
    assert [row["payload"]["name"] for row in parent_dispatches] == ["spawn_agent", "spawn_agent"]
    dispatched = {row["child"]: row["arguments"]["message"] for row in observations if row["kind"] == "Dispatch"}
    stops = {}
    child_events = []
    for label, msg in dispatched.items():
        tool_id = f"input-{label}"
        pre = one((row for row in hook_rows if row["configuredEvent"] == "PreToolUse"
                   and row["payload"].get("tool_use_id") == tool_id), f"pre {label}")
        post = one((row for row in hook_rows if row["configuredEvent"] == "PostToolUse"
                    and row["payload"].get("tool_use_id") == tool_id), f"post {label}")
        assert pre["payload"]["tool_input"]["message"] == post["payload"]["tool_input"]["message"] == msg
        assert pre["payload"]["turn_id"] == post["payload"]["turn_id"] == turn_ids[0]
        dispatch = references(parent_rows, parent_path, lambda row:
                              row["payload"].get("type") == "item_completed" and
                              row["payload"].get("item", {}).get("id") == tool_id)
        item = dispatch["payload"]["item"]
        child_id = item["agent_thread_id"] if mode == "v2" else one(item["receiver_thread_ids"], label)
        assert dispatch["payload"]["turn_id"] == turn_ids[0]
        start = one((row for row in hook_rows if row["configuredEvent"] == "SubagentStart"
                     and row["payload"]["agent_id"] == child_id), f"subagent start {label}")
        stop = one((row for row in hook_rows if row["configuredEvent"] == "SubagentStop"
                    and row["payload"]["agent_id"] == child_id), f"subagent stop {label}")
        stops[label] = stop
        for native_event in (start, stop):
            fields = native_event["payload"]
            assert fields["session_id"] == parent_id and Path(fields["transcript_path"]).name == parent_file.name
            assert fields["turn_id"] not in turn_ids and "timestamp" not in fields
        start_payload, stop_payload = start["payload"], stop["payload"]
        assert start_payload["turn_id"] == stop_payload["turn_id"]
        assert "prompt" not in start_payload and "tool_input" not in start_payload
        completion = one((row for row in observations if row["kind"] == "ChildCompleteReadBack"
                          and row["label"] == label), f"child task_complete {label}")
        reply = completion["payload"]["last_agent_message"]
        assert reply == stop_payload["last_assistant_message"]
        assert completion["payload"]["turn_id"] == stop_payload["turn_id"]
        assert Path(stop_payload["agent_transcript_path"]).name == completion["file"]
        assert stop["wallNs"] < int(datetime.fromisoformat(completion["timestamp"]).timestamp() * 1e9)
        child_prompts = [row["payload"] for row in hook_rows if row["configuredEvent"] == "UserPromptSubmit"
                         and row["payload"].get("agent_id") == child_id]
        assert [payload["prompt"] for payload in child_prompts] == ([msg] if mode == "v1" else [])
        assert msg not in record.read_text() and reply not in record.read_text()
        parent_reply_rows = [
            {"line": index, "type": row["payload"]["type"], "timestamp": row["timestamp"]}
            for index, row in enumerate(parent_rows, 1)
            if reply.splitlines()[0] in json.dumps(row["payload"], ensure_ascii=False)
        ]
        parent_completions = [
            {"line": index, "parentTurnId": row["payload"]["turn_id"], "timestamp": row["timestamp"]}
            for index, row in enumerate(parent_rows, 1)
            if row["payload"].get("item", {}).get("agent_thread_id") == child_id and
            row["payload"]["item"].get("kind") == "completed"
        ]
        if mode == "v2":
            completion_in_parent = one(parent_completions, label)
            assert completion_in_parent["parentTurnId"] == turn_ids[0]
            if label == "B":
                assert completion_in_parent["line"] > parent_turns[1]["started"]["line"]
                assert not parent_reply_rows
        child_events.append({
            "agent": label, "childThreadId": child_id, "childTurnId": stop_payload["turn_id"],
            "originalInput": msg, "reply": reply, "parentTurnId": turn_ids[0], "callId": tool_id,
            "toolHooks": {"name": post["payload"]["tool_name"], "pre": as_utc(pre["wallNs"]),
                          "post": as_utc(post["wallNs"]), "postToolResponse": json.loads(post["payload"]["tool_response"])},
            "dispatchRecord": {"file": parent_path, "line": dispatch["line"], "kind": item["type"],
                               "time": dispatch["timestamp"], "agentPath": item.get("agent_path")},
            "subagentStart": {"time": as_utc(start["wallNs"]), "keys": sorted(start_payload)},
            "subagentStop": {"time": as_utc(stop["wallNs"]), "keys": sorted(stop_payload)},
            "childTaskComplete": {"file": Path(stop_payload["agent_transcript_path"]).relative_to(directory / "state").as_posix(),
                                  "line": completion["line"], "time": completion["timestamp"]},
            "copyOfReplyInParentRollout": parent_reply_rows,
            "completionEventInParentRollout": parent_completions,
            "childPromptHookCount": len(child_prompts),
        })

    a, b = stops["A"], stops["B"]
    assert reads["first"]["wallNs"] < a["wallNs"] < prompt_hooks[1]["wallNs"] < b["wallNs"] < reads["second"]["wallNs"]
    second_started = parent_turns[1]["started"]["time"]
    assert as_utc(a["wallNs"]) < second_started < as_utc(b["wallNs"])
    gates = {row["label"]: row["wallNs"] for row in observations if row["kind"] == "GateRelease"}
    assert reads["first"]["wallNs"] < gates["A"] < a["wallNs"]
    assert prompt_hooks[1]["wallNs"] < gates["B"] < b["wallNs"] < gates["parent-second"]
    assert [row["configuredEvent"] for row in hook_rows
            if gates["A"] < row["wallNs"] < prompt_hooks[1]["wallNs"]] == ["SubagentStop"]
    assert [row["configuredEvent"] for row in hook_rows
            if gates["B"] < row["wallNs"] < gates["parent-second"]] == ["SubagentStop"]
    observation_seconds = (checkpoints["child-a-parent-idle"]["wallNs"] - a["wallNs"]) / 1e9
    return {
        "mode": mode, "install": {"version": install["version"], "sha256": install["hashes"],
                                  "trustedHooks": sorted(trust["hooks"])},
        "parentSessionId": parent_id, "parentRollout": parent_path,
        "record": reads["first"]["record"], "currentMainRecordMessageLabels": [
            line for line in record.read_text().splitlines() if line.startswith("### ")],
        "parentTurns": parent_turns, "children": child_events,
        "idleObservationSeconds": round(observation_seconds, 3),
        "recordSnapshots": {label: row["snapshot"] for label, row in checkpoints.items()
                            if label != "ordinary-control-done"},
        "ordinaryControl": {"sessionId": control_id, "record": reads["control"]["record"],
                            "recordReadBackAt": as_utc(reads["control"]["wallNs"]),
                            "recordSha256": reads["control"]["sha256"],
                            "record": reads["control"]["record"], "fullText": control.read_text()},
    }


runs = [main(mode) for mode in ("v2", "v1")]
assert runs[0]["install"]["sha256"] == runs[1]["install"]["sha256"]
print(json.dumps({"scenario": "两名业务子代理直接派发及跨轮回传的隔离宿主取证", "timeStandard": "UTC",
                  "source": "cf8def3:packages/skills/src/profile/conversation-recording",
                  "runSource": "真实 Linux Codex CLI 原生交互、隔离回环合成回复",
                  "runs": runs}, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
`}
PY
/usr/bin/python3 -B ${work}/extract.py ${work} > ${work}/observations-for-spec.json`,max_output_tokens:3700});text(r);

## 工具调用 `exec` · `call_0fa38cc19ad44a959cf2a8d55112109a`

- 来源定位：字节 1989690

~~~text
工作目录：/home/ena/workspace/ena/agent-foundry/feature/init
const work=load("timeline_work"), repo="/home/ena/workspace/ena/agent-foundry/feature/init";
const r=await tools.exec_command({cmd:`/usr/bin/python3 -B - ${work}/observations-for-spec.json <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
import json
import sys

source = Path(sys.argv[1])
evidence = json.loads(source.read_text())
runs = evidence["runs"]
hashes = runs[0]["install"]["sha256"]
evidence["installation"] = {
    "version": runs[0]["install"]["version"],
    "verifiedFileCount": len(hashes),
    "eventModuleSha256": hashes["recording/events.py"],
    "entrySha256": {name: digest for name, digest in hashes.items() if name.startswith("scripts/")},
}
for run in runs:
    assert run["install"]["sha256"] == hashes
    run["trustedHooks"] = run.pop("install")["trustedHooks"]
    snapshots = run.pop("recordSnapshots")
    windows = []
    for before, after in (
        ("parent-first-done", "child-a-parent-idle"),
        ("parent-second-started", "child-b-parent-second-active"),
    ):
        assert snapshots[before] == snapshots[after]
        windows.append({
            "before": before,
            "after": after,
            "fileCount": len(snapshots[before]),
            "mainSha256Before": snapshots[before][run["record"]][1],
            "mainSha256After": snapshots[after][run["record"]][1],
            "fileListHashAndMtimeEqual": True,
        })
    run["recordSnapshotComparison"] = windows
    run["ordinaryControl"].pop("fullText")
destination = Path("specs/skills/002-conversation-to-skill/analysis/acceptance/subagent-recording-timeline.json")
with destination.open("x", encoding="utf-8") as output:
    json.dump(evidence, output, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
    output.write("\\n")
print(destination)
PY`,workdir:repo,max_output_tokens:1900});text(r);

~~~

01:09:05.837 | 01:09:05.768 |\n+| 父日志记录第二用户轮开始 | 01:09:07.500 | 01:09:07.416 |\n+| 第二轮进行时收到 B 的 `SubagentStop` | 01:09:08.030 | 01:09:07.958 |\n+| 主记录第二轮回复已读回 | 01:09:08.718 | 01:09:08.742 |\n+\n+A 和 B 回传前后分别比较记录文件清单、哈希和修改时间，两组各自一致。当前记录入口生成的主记录包含两轮用户与主代理的消息。无子代理对照用同一安装独立启动，发起和回复各保存一次，未生成详情文件。 [结构化观测](acceptance/subagent-recording-timeline.json)\n+\n+## 字段来源\n+\n+| 字段 | 已核实的宿主来源 |\n+| --- | --- `transcript_path` 指向子线程记录。停止回调的 `transcript_path` 指向父会话记录，另有 `agent_transcript_path` 指向子线程记录。 |\n+| 发生时间 | 两种子代理回调均未携带事件时间字段。父日志提供派发时间，子线程 `task_complete` 提供宿主完成时间。实测停止回调先于子线程完成条目持久化，表中的回调接收时间来自探针。V2 的父日志还保留两次子代理完成事件，其中 B 的完成事件出现在第二轮开始后，归属仍指向原派发轮。 |\n+\n+## 证据边界\n+\n+本轮确认了直接派发时的父会话输入来源，以及父会话空闲和活跃时的子代理回复回调。正式主记录的交错写入和分隔仍待接入并复验 SC-010、SC-011。后续补充消息和 Code Mode 中的派发未在本轮实测。 ## 工具调用 `exec` · `call_d8194b5e93a141de82f3d4f63f242a20`

- 来源定位：字节 1998943

~~~text
工作目录：/home/ena/workspace/ena/agent-foundry/feature/init
const patch="*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: specs/skills/002-conversation-to-skill/analysis/subagent-recording-timeline.md\n" + String.raw`# 业务子代理跨轮时间线取证

2026-11-27 在两个临时 Git 仓库运行 Linux Codex CLI 0.172.0，分别使用 V1 和 V2 的直接派发工具。两个仓库都从维护源通过 \`install.py\` 安装记录 Skill，逐个比对 17 个文件的 SHA-256，再用原生界面完成项目和 Hook 信任。回环端点只控制合成输入及回复的发出时机，派发、线程、回调和落盘均由实际宿主执行。探针以额外的只读观测 Hook 取证。 [结构化观测](acceptance/subagent-recording-timeline.json)

## 实测顺序

第二轮的合成回复有单独的放行点，以便观察该轮进行时到达的子代理回复。父会话的工具调用只有两次 \`spawn_agent\`。表中时间均为 UTC，区分宿主日志时间、回调接收时间和文件读回时间。

| 事件 | V2 | V1 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 父日志记录派发 A | 01:09:03.861 | 01:09:03.872 |
| 父日志记录派发 B | 01:09:04.695 | 01:09:04.683 |
| 主记录首轮回复已读回，A 和 B 均尚未放行 | 01:09:05.575 | 01:09:05.545 |
| 父会话空闲时收到 A 的 \`SubagentStop\` | 01:09:05.837 | 01:09:05.768 |
| 父日志记录第二用户轮开始 | 01:09:07.500 | 01:09:07.416 |
| 第二轮进行时收到 B 的 \`SubagentStop\` | 01:09:08.030 | 01:09:07.958 |
| 主记录第二轮回复已读回 | 01:09:08.718 | 01:09:08.742 |

A 和 B 回传前后分别比较记录文件清单、哈希和修改时间，两组各自一致。当前记录入口生成的主记录包含两轮用户与主代理的消息。无子代理对照用同一安装独立启动，发起和回复各保存一次，未生成详情文件。 [结构化观测](acceptance/subagent-recording-timeline.json)

## 字段来源

| 字段 | 已核实的宿主来源 |
| --- | --- |
| 派发输入原文 | V1 和 V2 的父会话 \`PreToolUse\`、\`PostToolUse\` 均在 \`tool_input.message\` 提供原文，与两条含空行的合成输入逐字一致。 |
| 派发所属父轮与子线程 | 父会话工具回调提供父轮 \`turn_id\` 和 \`tool_use_id\`。V1 的工具回包带 \`agent_id\`，父日志的 \`CollabAgentToolCall.id\` 也可用工具调用 ID 关联子线程。V2 的工具回包给出代理路径，父日志的 \`SubAgentActivity\` 启动事件以同一工具调用 ID 关联 \`agent_thread_id\`。 |
| 子代理身份和回复全文 | \`SubagentStop\` 直接提供 \`agent_id\` 和 \`last_assistant_message\`。两组四条回复的原文与对应子线程的宿主 \`task_complete\` 逐字一致。V2 的父日志在所测两轮内出现了 A 的回复副本，B 的全文当时可从回调取得。 |
| 子代理回调归属 | \`SubagentStart\` 和 \`SubagentStop\` 的 \`session_id\` 是父会话 ID，\`turn_id\` 是子线程轮次。启动回调的 \`transcript_path\` 指向子线程记录。停止回调的 \`transcript_path\` 指向父会话记录，另有 \`agent_transcript_path\` 指向子线程记录。 |
| 发生时间 | 两种子代理回调均未携带事件时间字段。父日志提供派发时间，子线程 \`task_complete\` 提供宿主完成时间。实测停止回调先于子线程完成条目持久化，表中的回调接收时间来自探针。V2 的父日志还保留两次子代理完成事件，其中 B 的完成事件出现在第二轮开始后，归属仍指向原派发轮。 |

## 证据边界

本轮确认了直接派发时的父会话输入来源，以及父会话空闲和活跃时的子代理回复回调。正式主记录的交错写入和分隔仍待接入并复验 SC-010、SC-011。后续补充消息和 Code Mode 中的派发未在本轮实测。 [验收标准](../spec.md#measurable-outcomes) · [早期子代理取证](content-source.md#子代理活动及父会话可见字段)

## feature/init...origin/feature/init [ahead 1]
 M .codex/hooks.json
 M .specify/memory/conversation-recording/records/2026-11-26/01a2174d-dd90-74b1-8444-b2b62149b17b.md

     1	# 业务子代理跨轮时间线取证
     2	
     3	2026-11-27 在两个临时 Git 仓库运行 Linux Codex CLI 0.172.0，分别使用 V1 和 V2 的直接派发工具。两个仓库都从维护源通过 `install.py` 安装记录 Skill，逐个比对 17 个文件的 SHA-256，再用原生界面完成项目和 Hook 信任。回环端点只控制合成输入及回复的发出时机，派发、线程、回调和落盘均由实际宿主执行。探针以额外的只读观测 Hook 取证。 [结构化观测](acceptance/subagent-recording-timeline.json)
     4	
     5	## 实测顺序
     6	
     7	第二轮的合成回复有单独的放行点，以便观察该轮进行时到达的子代理回复。父会话的工具调用只有两次 `spawn_agent`。表中时间均为 UTC，区分宿主日志时间、回调接收时间和文件读回时间。
     8	
     9	| 事件                                    | V2           | V1           |
    10	| --------------------------------------- | ------------ | ------------ |
    11	| 父日志记录派发 A                        | 01:09:03.861 | 01:09:03.872 |
    12	| 父日志记录派发 B                        | 01:09:04.695 | 01:09:04.683 |
    13	| 主记录首轮回复已读回，A 和 B 均尚未放行 | 01:09:05.575 | 01:09:05.545 |
    14	| 父会话空闲时收到 A 的 `SubagentStop`    | 01:09:05.837 | 01:09:05.768 |
    15	| 父日志记录第二用户轮开始                | 01:09:07.500 | 01:09:07.416 |
    16	| 第二轮进行时收到 B 的 `SubagentStop`    | 01:09:08.030 | 01:09:07.958 |
    17	| 主记录第二轮回复已读回                  | 01:09:08.718 | 01:09:08.742 |
    18	
    19	A 和 B 回传前后分别比较记录文件清单、哈希和修改时间，两组各自一致。当前记录入口生成的主记录包含两轮用户与主代理的消息。无子代理对照用同一安装独立启动，发起和回复各保存一次，未生成详情文件。 [结构化观测](acceptance/subagent-recording-timeline.json)
    20	
    21	## 字段来源
    22	
    23	| 字段                 | 已核实的宿主来源                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
    24	| -------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
    25	| 派发输入原文         | V1 和 V2 的父会话 `PreToolUse`、`PostToolUse` 均在 `tool_input.message` 提供原文，与两条含空行的合成输入逐字一致。                                                                                                                                                |
    26	| 派发所属父轮与子线程 | 父会话工具回调提供父轮 `turn_id` 和 `tool_use_id`。V1 的工具回包带 `agent_id`，父日志的 `CollabAgentToolCall.id` 也可用工具调用 ID 关联子线程。V2 的工具回包给出代理路径，父日志的 `SubAgentActivity` 启动事件以同一工具调用 ID 关联 `agent_thread_id`。          |
    27	| 子代理身份和回复全文 | `SubagentStop` 直接提供 `agent_id` 和 `last_assistant_message`。两组四条回复的原文与对应子线程的宿主 `task_complete` 逐字一致。V2 的父日志在所测两轮内出现了 A 的回复副本，B 的全文当时可从回调取得。                                                             |
    28	| 子代理回调归属       | `SubagentStart` 和 `SubagentStop` 的 `session_id` 是父会话 ID，`turn_id` 是子线程轮次。启动回调的 `transcript_path` 指向子线程记录。停止回调的 `transcript_path` 指向父会话记录，另有 `agent_transcript_path` 指向子线程记录。                                    |
    29	| 发生时间             | 两种子代理回调均未携带事件时间字段。父日志提供派发时间，子线程 `task_complete` 提供宿主完成时间。实测停止回调先于子线程完成条目持久化，表中的回调接收时间来自探针。V2 的父日志还保留两次子代理完成事件，其中 B 的完成事件出现在第二轮开始后，归属仍指向原派发轮。 |
    30	
    31	## 证据边界
    32	
    33	本轮确认了直接派发时的父会话输入来源，以及父会话空闲和活跃时的子代理回复回调。正式主记录的交错写入和分隔仍待接入并复验 SC-010、SC-011。后续补充消息和 Code Mode 中的派发未在本轮实测。 [验收标准](../spec.md#measurable-outcomes) · [早期子代理取证](content-source.md#子代理活动及父会话可见字段)

~~~

## 工具调用 `exec` · `call_427fc13e10774d69ba4af09f10f40e84`

- 来源定位：字节 2081701

~~~text
工作目录：/home/ena/workspace/ena/agent-foundry/feature/init
const repo="/home/ena/workspace/ena/agent-foundry/feature/init", work=load("timeline_work");
const verify=await Promise.allSettled([
 tools.exec_command({
  cmd:`/usr/bin/python3 -B - ${work}/observations-readback.json <<'PY'
import json, re, subprocess, sys
from hashlib import sha256
from pathlib import Path

directory = Path("specs/skills/002-conversation-to-skill/analysis")
markdown = directory / "subagent-recording-timeline.md"
evidence = directory / "acceptance/subagent-recording-timeline.json"
document = markdown.read_text()
stored = json.loads(evidence.read_text())
raw = json.loads(Path(sys.argv[1]).read_text())
assert stored["source"] == raw["source"]
revision, source_path = stored["source"].split(":", 1)
expected = {"recording/events.py": stored["installation"]["eventModuleSha256"],
            **stored["installation"]["entrySha256"]}
assert stored["installation"]["verifiedFileCount"] == 17
for filename, digest in expected.items():
    tracked = subprocess.check_output(["git", "show", f"{revision}:{source_path}/{filename}"])
    assert sha256(tracked).hexdigest() == digest == sha256((Path(source_path) / filename).read_bytes()).hexdigest()

for run, original in zip(stored["runs"], raw["runs"], strict=True):
    assert run["mode"] == original["mode"]
    for label in ("parentSessionId", "parentRollout", "record", "parentTurns", "children"):
        assert run[label] == original[label]
    first, second = run["parentTurns"]
    alpha, beta = run["children"]
    actual_times = [alpha["dispatchRecord"]["time"], beta["dispatchRecord"]["time"], first["recordReadBackAt"],
                    alpha["subagentStop"]["time"], second["started"]["time"], beta["subagentStop"]["time"],
                    second["recordReadBackAt"]]
    assert actual_times == sorted(actual_times)
    for time in actual_times:
        assert time[11:23] in document
    for comparison in run["recordSnapshotComparison"]:
        before = original["recordSnapshots"][comparison["before"]]
        after = original["recordSnapshots"][comparison["after"]]
        assert before == after and comparison["fileCount"] == len(before)
        assert comparison["mainSha256Before"] == comparison["mainSha256After"] == before[run["record"]][1]
    assert run["ordinaryControl"]["recordSha256"] == original["ordinaryControl"]["recordSha256"]
for link in re.findall(r"\\]\\(([^)#]+)(?:#[^)]*)?\\)", document):
    assert (markdown.parent / link).is_file(), link
for file in (markdown, evidence):
    data = file.read_bytes()
    assert data.endswith(b"\\n") and all(line.rstrip() == line for line in data.splitlines())
print("V1、V2 的事件顺序、快照、时间表、安装来源与本地链接均已核对")
PY`,workdir:repo,max_output_tokens:2800}),
 tools.exec_command({
  cmd:"git status --short --branch; git diff --stat -- specs/skills/002-conversation-to-skill packages/skills/src/profile/conversation-recording; nl -ba specs/skills/002-conversation-to-skill/analysis/subagent-recording-timeline.md | sed -n '1,55p'",workdir:repo,max_output_tokens:4600})
]);verify.forEach((r,i)=>{if(r.status==="fulfilled")text({i,...r.value});else text({i,error:String(r.reason)});});
